Police appeal after £538 worth of toothbrush heads stolen from Tesco in Dibden Police are appealing for information after 38 toothbrush heads, worth £538, were reportedly stolen from a Tesco store in Dibden. Hampshire & Isle of Wight Constabulary is seeking to identify a person who may be able to assist with its investigation into the reported shoplifting at the Tesco store on Sizer Way. The incident is reported to have taken place between 9.15pm and 10.10pm on Monday, 9 November. Officers have released an image of a person they would like to speak to in connection with the incident. The individual is described as white, aged between their 30s and 40s, and approximately 5ft 7ins tall. Police are urging the person pictured, or anyone who recognises them, to come forward. Anyone who witnessed the incident or noticed anything suspicious in the area at the time is also being asked to contact officers. Information can be provided by calling 101 or by submitting a report through theregulatory
